*Xi Yang,Guodong Liu,Chuqin Li,Fan Wu,Ergin Soysal,Min Jiang,Xing He,Jiang Bian,Yi Guo,Shams Zaman,Thomas Fuchs,Todd Sanger,Yonghui Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了在生物医学数据到文本生成任务中训练小语言模型（SLM）的多种后训练方法，包括SFT、DPO、ORPO和GRPO，基于Qwen模型在药品说明书数据集上进行实验。结果表明：对齐后的SLM优于GPT-5；ORPO优于SFT基线；GRPO在跨数据集泛化方面表现最为鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将复杂的生物医学数据转化为患者友好型叙述是生物医学信息学的核心需求，但目前对如何通过后训练方法有效提升小语言模型在此类专门化任务上的表现缺乏系统比较。

Method: 基于Qwen架构的小语言模型，在药品说明书数据集上应用SFT、DPO、ORPO和GRPO四种后训练方法，并在openFDA药物标签数据上评估跨数据集泛化能力。采用ROUGE等词法重叠指标和语义相似度指标进行评价。

Result: (1) 对齐后的SLM优于GPT-5；(2) ORPO优于SFT基线；(3) GRPO在跨数据集泛化上表现最优。

Conclusion: 后训练对齐方法能显著提升SLM在生物医学数据到文本生成任务中的表现，其中GRPO在跨数据集泛化方面最具优势，为专门领域小模型的部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Translating complex biomedical data into patient-friendly narratives is central to modern biomedical informatics. This study presents a comparative analysis of training small language models (SLMs) in specialized biomedical datato-text generation tasks. We explore widely adopted post-training methods including supervised fine-tuning (SFT), direct preference optimization (DPO), odds ratio preference optimization (ORPO), and group relative policy optimization (GRPO) with Qwen-based SLMs on a medicine package leaflets dataset. To assess cross-dataset generalizability, we also curated drug label data from openFDA. We evaluate models using both standard lexical overlap metrics like ROUGE as well as semantic similarity measures. Across our experiments, the results show that (1) the aligned SLMs outperform proprietary models like GPT-5; (2) ORPO outperforms the SFTbaselines; (3) GRPO yields the most robust cross-dataset performance among the alignment methods tested as well as GPT-5.

</details>


### [104] [When Rubrics Change: Cross-Rubric Generalization for Critical Thinking Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13433)
*Nischal Ashok Kumar,Payu Wittawatolarn,Sana Kang,Marisa C. Peczuh,Blair Lehman,Ryan Baker,Caitlin Mills,Sherry Lachman,Ruochen Sun,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自动作文评分中的跨评分标准泛化问题，提出基于大语言模型的微调框架，通过引入评分标准无关的中间表示（traits）和目标作文监督信号，使模型能泛化到训练时未见过的评分标准。在扩展了多维度批判性思维标签的数据集上，traits机制在最具挑战性的设置下（目标评分标准和目标作文均未见）将macro F1提升5.0%，且最佳开源模型超越GPT-5-mini提示方法2.1%，与GPT-5差距仅1.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AES研究主要关注跨提示泛化（作文题目变化而评分标准固定），但实践中教育者经常修改或引入新评分标准来评估作文的不同维度。现有方法无法有效应对这种评分标准变化的情况，因此需要研究跨评分标准泛化问题。

Method: 提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的微调框架，包含两个核心组件：1）评分标准无关的中间表示（traits），捕捉作文的通用特征；2）在训练阶段对目标作文在已见评分标准下的监督信号。两者结合使模型学会从已见评分标准向未见评分标准迁移知识。

Result: 在带有多个评分标准定义的批判性思维标签的数据集上，traits在目标评分标准和目标作文均未见的困难设置下比无traits基线提升5.0% macro F1。增加目标作文监督进一步提升性能，最佳微调开源Llama模型超越GPT-5-mini提示方法2.1% macro F1，仅落后GPT-5 1.9%。

Conclusion: 基于traits的中间结构和受控的监督信号能有效提升模型向未见评分标准的泛化能力，为实际教育场景中评分标准动态变化问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated essay scoring (AES) research has largely focused on cross-prompt generalization, where essays from unseen prompts are scored while the scoring criteria are typically held constant. In practice, however, educators may revise or even introduce new rubrics in their scoring task, to evaluate different aspects of essays. We study cross-rubric generalization: training on essays labeled under one set of rubrics and evaluating on previously unseen rubrics, which target different aspects of the essay. We use a Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning framework with two components: rubric-agnostic intermediate representations, called traits, and target-essay supervision under seen rubrics during training. On an AES dataset augmented with multiple rubric-defined labels of student critical thinking skills, we find that traits improve macro F1 by 5.0% over a baseline without traits in the hardest setting, where both target rubrics and target essays are unseen during training. We further find that increasing target-essay supervision improves performance, with our best fine-tuned open-source Llama-based model outperforming GPT-5-mini prompting by 2.1% macro F1 and trailing GPT-5 by 1.9%. These results show that trait-based intermediate structure and controlled supervision improve generalization to unseen rubrics.

</details>


### [105] [Live Gurbani Tracking: A Benchmark and Reference System for Captioning Sikh Kirtan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13457)
*Karanbir Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对锡克教Kirtan（从《古鲁·格兰特·萨希卜》中连续唱诵经文）提出了一个闭词汇实时字幕基准和参考系统。任务形式化为在每个时间t预测(shabad_id, line_idx)或空值，按实时/离线（因果/全音频访问）和盲/先知（shabad身份发现/已知）两个正交轴组织问题空间。发布了包含4个手工标注录音×3个冷启动偏移=12个评估案例（约57分钟）的v1基准，以及一个计算帧精度的评分器。参考系统（微调IndicConformer→模糊匹配器→状态机；INT8 ONNX；在一个Apple Silicon核心上RTF约0.05）在最难变体（实时×盲）上达到57.9%的整体帧精度（12个案例中10/12正确锁定shabad）。与三个简单基线比较，并讨论了为何标准ASR指标不适合此任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锡克教Kirtan的实时字幕显示要求每行必须是经文的逐字准确行，拼写错误被认为宗教上不合适，因此标准的开放词汇歌词转录方法不适用。需要为这一闭词汇问题建立基准和系统。

Method: 任务形式化为在每个时间t预测(shabad_id, line_idx)或空值；按实时/离线与盲/先知两个正交轴组织为2×2问题空间。参考系统采用微调120M IndicConformer→模糊匹配器→状态机流水线，INT8 ONNX优化，在Apple Silicon上RTF~0.05。

Result: 在4个手工标注录音×3个冷启动偏移=12个评估案例（~57分钟）上，参考系统在最难变体（实时×盲）上达到57.9%整体帧精度，12个案例中10/12正确锁定shabad。发布了基准、评分器和参考系统。

Conclusion: 建立了锡克教Kirtan实时字幕的闭词汇基准和参考系统，证明该任务可行，并指出标准ASR指标（WER/CER）衡量的是转录精度而非显示精度，不适用于此任务。

Abstract: We present a benchmark and reference system for live captioning of Sikh Kirtan - the continuous, sung recitation of verses from the Sri Guru Granth Sahib Ji (SGGS). Unlike open-vocabulary lyrics transcription, Kirtan captioning is a closed-vocabulary problem: every displayed line must be an exact, word-for-word line from the canonical scripture, because displaying misspelled Gurmukhi is considered religiously inappropriate. We formalize the task as predicting, at every time t, a pair (shabad_id, line_idx) or null, and organize the problem space into a 2x2 matrix along two orthogonal axes: live vs. offline (causal vs. full-audio access) and blind vs. oracle (shabad identity discovered vs. given). We release v1 of the benchmark - 4 hand-annotated Kirtan recordings x 3 cold-start offsets = 12 evaluation cases, ~57 minutes of scored audio - together with a scorer that computes frame accuracy at 1s resolution over a scored region, with a 1s collar and gap-tolerant scoring at segment boundaries. We describe a reference system (fine-tuned 120M IndicConformer -> fuzzy matcher -> state machine; INT8 ONNX; RTF ~0.05 on one Apple Silicon core) that achieves 57.9% overall frame accuracy across all 12 cases (10/12 correct shabad locks) on the hardest variant (live x blind). We compare against three trivial baselines (empty, shifted-5s, perfect) and discuss why standard ASR metrics (WER/CER) measure transcription accuracy rather than the display accuracy this task requires. The benchmark, reference system, and a live deployment are released under permissive licenses to facilitate further improvements.

</details>


### [106] [DevicesWorld: Benchmarking Cross-Device Agents in Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13465)
*Huatao Li,Xinwei Geng,Yuheng Wang,Yutong Li,Runde Yang,Hantao Chen,Shu Yao,Jingru Fan,Xuhui Ren,Yuanyuan Zhao,Fei Huang,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DevicesWorld，一个包含6,140个任务的大规模可执行跨设备协作基准测试，覆盖手机、桌面和物联网三类设备环境。当前最佳LLM智能体系统在该基准上成功率仅12.5%，揭示了跨设备信息获取、界面操作和设备混淆等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试大多聚焦于单一设备环境（如手机、桌面或智能家居），无法评估智能体在异构设备间获取和整合信息、完成跨设备端到端任务的能力，而真实用户需求往往需要多设备协同操作。

Method: DevicesWorld整合了移动、桌面和IoT三类设备环境，构建统一跨设备交互与评估框架，包含6,140个任务。每个任务包含自然语言用户目标、参与设备及初始状态、可执行动作、基于规则的验证器和清理流程。采用多阶段构建与质量控制管线确保任务贴近真实用户需求，并支持从设备状态和生成文件自动验证结果。

Result: 在五种前沿LLM智能体系统上的评估中，最佳方法成功率仅为12.5%。失败案例中约28.7%满足至少一个评分条件但仍未完全完成任务。轨迹分析显示智能体在信息获取或界面操作中陷入困境，混淆源设备和目标设备，或在所有条件满足前终止。

Conclusion: DevicesWorld将跨设备协作操作转化为可执行、可复现且具有诊断价值的评估问题，为研究可靠的跨设备智能体提供了重要基准。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have rapidly improved at operating individual digital environments such as mobile applications, desktop systems, and smart homes. However, real-world user goals often span multiple devices: information may come from a phone, be processed on a desktop, and the result may need to appear on another device. Most existing benchmarks center on a single dominant execution environment, making it difficult to evaluate whether agents can acquire and integrate information across heterogeneous devices and complete end-to-end tasks with cross-device dependencies. We introduce DevicesWorld, a large-scale executable benchmark for cross-device collaborative operation. DevicesWorld contains 6,140 tasks and integrates three classes of device environments -- mobile, desktop, and IoT -- into a unified cross-device interaction and evaluation framework. Each task defines a natural-language user goal, participating devices and initial states, executable actions, rule-based verifiers, and a cleanup procedure. A multi-stage construction and quality-control pipeline keeps tasks close to realistic user needs while allowing final outcomes to be automatically verified from device states and generated files. We evaluate five frontier LLM-agent systems on a fixed evaluation set. All methods achieve low success rates, with the best reaching only 12.5%. Among failed runs, about 28.7% satisfy at least one scoring condition yet still fail the full task. Trajectories show that agents become stuck acquiring information or manipulating interfaces, confuse source and output devices, or terminate before all conditions are jointly satisfied. DevicesWorld turns cross-device collaborative operation into an executable, reproducible, and diagnostically useful evaluation problem for research on reliable cross-device agents.

</details>


### [107] [MyAG: A Graph-Based Framework for Designing and Analyzing Composable LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13474)
*Zhisong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MyAG是一个基于图抽象的可组合LLM代理系统框架，通过组件图、工作流图和搜索图三层分离设计，支持灵活复用、层次化组合和可视化监控，已在多个代理应用中验证其有效性和性能-效率权衡分析能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理系统设计缺乏统一的抽象框架，难以灵活复用组件、分析性能-效率权衡以及可视化执行过程。

Method: 提出三种图抽象：组件图（代理、环境、模块）、工作流图（执行控制）、搜索图（运行时执行），支持递归系统节点实现层次化组合，并提供监控和可视化工具。

Result: 在代表性代理应用中验证了框架支持灵活设计和性能-效率权衡分析。

Conclusion: MyAG框架有效实现了可组合LLM代理系统的设计与分析，已开源。

Abstract: We present MyAG, a graph-based framework for designing and analyzing composable LLM agent systems. Our framework separates agent system construction into three graph abstractions: a component graph for agents, environments, and modules; a workflow graph for execution control; and a search graph for runtime execution. This separation allows users to flexibly reuse the same components with different strategies. We further support hierarchical composition through recursive system nodes and provide monitoring and visualization tools for inspecting agent execution. Experiments on representative agent applications show that our framework supports flexible agent system design and helps analyze performance-efficiency tradeoffs. Our framework is publicly available and fully open-source.

</details>


### [108] [Cost-Pragmatic Quality Gating and Selection-Fusion Multi-Model Combiners for BioASQ Phases A+ and B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13551)
*Dima Galat,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了BioASQ Task 14B 2026系统的两项核心设计：一是在首轮检索薄弱时采用成本-实用主义的重检索策略，二是将多模型集成增益分解为选择组件和融合组件，并据此选择最优聚合器。系统在多个榜单上取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决两个关键设计问题：首轮检索质量不足时如何高效地触发重检索，以及如何组合多个语言模型的答案以获得最优性能。

Method: 检索阶段：采用混合首轮检索（密集BGE + BM25 + RRF）和智能体驱动的检索流水线，并通过BGE交叉编码器质量门控筛选弱支持问题，执行选择性重检索。回答阶段：将多模型集成增益分解为选择组件（受限于每个问题的oracle）和融合组件（聚合器可超越oracle），据此预测不同聚合器在不同指标上的表现。

Result: 在Task 12B 2024验证集上，成本-实用主义重检索策略在列表F1和列表精确率上显著优于严格基线，重检索成本降低12%。在13B测试集上，列表F1提升了+0.132。在Task 13B 2025中，同义词联合解析器在所有头部上赢得列表召回率，但GPT-5.5单独保持列表F1领先。在Task 14B 2026初步榜单中，团队在8个（阶段×批次）榜单中的3个综合精确聚合上排名第一，赢得4个问题类型单元，并在Phase B b3 ideal中获得第1名。

Conclusion: 成本-实用主义的重检索策略在成本与性能之间取得了更好的平衡；多模型集成的选择-融合分解理论可以有效指导聚合器的选择，但不同指标对两种组件的依赖不同，需针对性设计。

Abstract: We describe our BioASQ Task 14B 2026 system. The work centers on two design decisions: how aggressively to re-retrieve when first-stage retrieval is weak, and how to combine multiple language-model answers. Retrieval unions two parallel pipelines - a hybrid first stage (dense BGE + BM25 + RRF, reaching R@200 = 99.3% on the BioASQ-13b historical archive) and an agent-driven pipeline that decomposes the question over PubMed, Europe PMC, and iCite - with a BGE cross-encoder quality gate flagging weakly-supported questions for selective re-retrieval. On Task 12B 2024 validation, a cost-pragmatic re-retrieval policy beats a skill-strict baseline significantly on list F1 and list precision, at 12% lower re-retrieval cost. Holding prompt and model fixed across val and test 13B (different question sets), list F1 rises by +0.132 absolute on the BioASQ-released gold-input pool, consistent with substantial retrieval-side headroom. For Phase B answering we decompose multi-model ensemble lift into a selection component bounded by the per-question oracle and a fusion component that aggregators can exceed. The decomposition predicts before any experiment that LLM-as-judge wins on selection-dominated metrics (yes/no, multi-reference ROUGE) but is structurally insufficient on the recall component of fusion-friendly metrics (factoid rank-1, list recall). On Task 13B 2025 our synonym-union resolver wins list recall on every head, while GPT-5.5 solo retains the list-F1 lead because the resolver's wider item set costs precision. On the Task 14B 2026 preliminary leaderboard our team places first on the combined-exact aggregate on three of the eight (phase x batch) leaderboards, wins four individual question-type cells, and takes #1 on Phase B b3 ideal.

</details>


### [109] [Graded Entity-Familiarity Readouts in Language Models: Polish Adaptation, Cross-Language Robustness, and Refusal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13568)
*Grzegorz Brzezinka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在12个指令微调模型中，通过最终提示词token的激活值提取“熟悉度探针”，证明模型在生成前就能分级评估对实体的熟悉程度，且该表征独立于是否选择拒答的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型是否能在生成答案之前，推断自己是否“知道”某个实体，从而实现更可靠的拒答机制，减少幻觉。

Method: 构建包含1,440个波兰语实体（覆盖4个领域、10个Wikipedia页面浏览十分位数及虚构实体）的数据集；从12个指令微调模型（Bielik、PLLuM、Gemma-4、Qwen3家族）的最终提示词token提取激活值，训练熟悉度探针；在Gemma-4-12B上进行单层干预，改变拒答行为；对比生成前与生成后的拒答门控性能。

Result: 所有模型家族中，熟悉度探针都能区分真实与虚构实体；在波兰语适配模型中，探针分数与实体流行度正相关（Spearman ρ 0.28-0.57），远高于非适配模型（≤0.11）；将问题语言从波兰语改为英语后，AUROC保持96-101%；在Gemma-4-12B上，单层干预可单调调节拒答率（知名实体0.24→1.00，未知实体0.73→0.00）；生成前探针在拒答门控中具有竞争力，但生成后检测器更优。

Conclusion: 模型存在分层级的生成前实体熟悉度表征，且熟悉度表征与是否执行拒答的策略是分离的。

Abstract: Can a language model estimate its familiarity with an entity before generating an answer? We study activations at the final prompt token in twelve instruction-tuned models from the Bielik, PLLuM, Gemma-4, and Qwen3 families, using a new dataset of 1,440 Polish entities spanning four domains and ten Wikipedia-pageview deciles, plus fabricated controls. Familiarity-probe scores separate real from fabricated entities in every family; in the Polish-adapted Bielik and PLLuM families they additionally track entity popularity (model-mean Spearman $ρ$ 0.28-0.57, versus at most 0.11 in Gemma-4 and Qwen3), a pattern more strongly associated with Polish adaptation than with parameter count in this model sample. In a paired experiment on two families, probes retain 96-101% of within-language AUROC when the Polish question stem is replaced with an English one around unchanged entity names, showing robustness to prompt language in this setting. In Gemma-4-12B, the only model that natively refuses, adding a one-dimensional familiarity direction at a single layer moves refusal rates monotonically in both directions (0.24 to 1.00 on well-known entities; 0.73 to 0.00 on unknown ones). Finally, a calibrated familiarity probe is competitive among pre-generation abstention gates, although post-generation detectors better predict behavioral error on average. These results support a graded pre-generation entity-familiarity readout, and a separation between representational familiarity and the policy that converts it into abstention.

</details>


### [110] [Memory as a Controlled Process: Learned Adaptive Memory Management for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13591)
*Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Yuchen Wu,Levina Li,Dong Liu,Xiao Liang,Rui Sun,Yubei Li,Edward Sun,Haozheng Luo,Zhaolu Kang,Aylin Caliskan,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MemCon框架，将记忆操作建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过无预训练、轻量级上下文赌博机在线学习自适应记忆策略，在6个基准测试中提升任务成功率最高15.2%，同时减少5-20%令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的记忆系统依赖固定手工启发式规则访问记忆，但最优记忆行为本质上依赖于上下文（如早期阶段应最小化检索、重复目标应重用计划、卡顿时应重新检索等），静态记忆视角是代理学习的核心瓶颈。

Method: 将记忆操作建模为马尔可夫决策过程，学习在线策略自适应决定何时、检索什么、检索多少、何时注入提炼计划、何时整合或遗忘。采用轻量级表格上下文赌博机算法（UCB探索），无需预训练和额外LLM调用，从逐任务二元反馈中学习，且后端无关。

Result: 在6个基准测试、3个代理框架、3个LLM骨干上，相比多个记忆基线，任务成功率提升最高15.2%，令牌消耗减少5-20%。

Conclusion: 动态、自适应的记忆策略显著优于固定启发式规则，MemCon提供了一种高效、通用、轻量的解决方案来优化LLM代理的记忆行为。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory systems to accumulate experience across tasks. Yet nearly all existing approaches, from graph-structured memories to reflective insight stores, access memory through fixed, hand-designed heuristics. We argue that this static view of memory is a core bottleneck for agentic learning because optimal memory behavior is fundamentally context-dependent. The early stages of the tasks, benefit from minimal retrieval because memory is sparse; recurring goal types benefit from plan reuse rather than generic nearest-neighbor lookup; stuck agents benefit from re-retrieval with alternative queries; and across long task streams, the memory store itself must be consolidated and pruned to remain useful. We present Memory as a Controlled Process (MemCon), a framework that models memory operations as a Markov Decision Process and learns an online policy that adaptively decides when, what, and how much to retrieve, when to inject a distilled plan, and when to consolidate or forget. MemCon is backend-agnostic: it wraps any existing memory implementation, learns from task-by-task binary feedback with no pretraining and no additional LLM calls, and uses a lightweight tabular contextual bandit with UCB exploration that converges within tens of tasks. Across 6 benchmarks, 3 agent frameworks, and 3 LLM backbones, MemCon consistently outperforms multiple memory baselines by up to 15.2 points in task success while reducing token consumption by 5--20%.

</details>


### [111] [Analogical Deep Research: Retrieving and Integrating Historical Analogies for Foresight Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13602)
*Yongqiang Chen,Guangyi Chen,Yuewen Sun,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了类比深度研究（ADR）任务及首个基准ADR-bench，发现LLM代理在寻找历史类比时因匹配表面特征而非深层机制而表现不佳。基于理论分析提出机制对齐和跨类比确认两个原则，并设计了因果类比研究员（CANA）框架，通过结构分解表示和结构反馈提升历史类比生成质量，在ADR-bench上超越现有深度研究方法，性能提升高达10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在进行前瞻分析时，难以有效发现和利用历史类比这一强大工具，因为模型倾向于匹配表面特征而非深层因果机制。作者希望系统性地研究LLM代理在历史类比推理方面的能力瓶颈，并提出有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出了CANA（Causal Analogical Researcher）框架，包含两个核心原则：机制对齐（mechanism alignment）和跨类比确认（cross-analogy confirmation）。采用结构分解表示（structural decomposition representation）来建模事件的因果结构，并结合结构反馈（structural feedback）进行反思性改进，以提升历史类比的识别和整合能力。

Result: CANA在历史类比生成任务上带来高达10%的性能提升，在ADR-bench基准上超越了最先进的深度研究代理。案例研究（包括正在发生的事件）验证了CANA在利用历史类比进行前瞻分析方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 历史类比的发现是一个因果问题，需要理解事件发生的深层机制而非表面特征。结构分解表示和结构反馈是提升LLM代理类比推理能力的关键方法，为未来基于类比的前瞻分析研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Systematic comparisons between current situations and structurally similar past events in the historical, i.e., historical analogies, is among the most powerful tools for foresight analysis. In this work, we present a new task called Analogical Deep Research (ADR) to Large Language Model (LLM) agents and construct the first ADR benchmark ADR-bench to study whether LLM agents are able to find and leverage historical analogies when doing foresight analysis. Our investigation reveals a key obstacle: LLM agents are poor at finding analogies because they match on surface features rather than underlying mechanisms. We argue that ADR is inherently a causal question as it requires understanding why the event occurred. Based on our theoretical analysis, we propose two principles required for ADR, including the mechanism alignment and cross-analogy confirmation. Built upon our theoretical results, we propose a new agentic framework called Causal Analogical Researcher (CANA) that guides LLMs to find and integrate historical analogies. CANA incorporates a simple yet effective structural decomposition representation, and integrates structural feedback for reflective improvements of historical analogy identification and integration. We show that CANA brings up to 10% improvements in historical analogy generation, and surpasses the state-of-the-art deep research agents in the ADR-bench. Case studies with the ongoing events confirm the effectiveness of CANA in leveraging historical analogies.

</details>


### [112] [Self-Evolving Agent Harnesses via Gated Semantic Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13683)
*Xiaotian Luo,Fengxingyu Wang,Chuanrui Hu,Dizhan Xue,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种自进化智能体框架，将提议变更（LLM生成）与信用分配（确定性代码）分离，通过基于病理的GSME存档和密封测试验证，在冻结模型上实现+9到+15.5pp的性能提升，且增益可泛化而非过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的实际任务性能很大程度上取决于其外围框架（提示词、注入知识、运行时控制、配置）而非模型本身。在部署中，框架是唯一可操作的杠杆，因此自动化改进框架是提升性能的自然途径。困难不在于生成变更，而在于确定哪些变更真正有效——自生成的反馈存在噪声，表面上的增益可能是测量伪影或过拟合的结果。

Method: 提出自进化智能体框架：(1) 语言模型负责诊断失败并提出补丁；(2) 所有采样、测量和显著性检验由确定性代码负责，确保可信任；(3) 补丁被存入基于（WHERE x WHY）病理的GSME（门控分类质量-多样性存档），而非基于任务，形成抗过拟合归纳偏置；(4) 泛化能力通过密封测试集评估，仅在进化完成后计分。

Result: 在七个领域使用冻结的开源模型，框架在密封测试上获得+9到+15.5pp的增益，且保留了训练增益的86-147%，证明泛化而非过拟合。获胜补丁追踪的是模型的主导病理而非模型大小或家族；更换模型会改变病理和补丁，但同一病理-补丁匹配在两个模型家族中重复出现。

Conclusion: 真正可迁移的是诊断-信用分配循环本身，而非任何特定框架配置。

Abstract: An LLM agent's real-task performance is shaped as much by the harness around its model as by the frozen model itself: its prompts, injected knowledge, runtime control, and configuration. In deployment the harness is often the only lever available, so improving it automatically is the natural way to raise performance without touching the weights. The hard part is not generating changes but knowing which one truly helped. Self-generated feedback is noisy, and an apparent gain can be a measurement artifact or an edit that merely overfits the tasks it was tuned on. We present a self-evolving agent-harness framework that separates proposing changes from crediting them: a language model diagnoses failures and proposes patches, while all sampling, measurement, and significance testing are owned by deterministic code, so every credited improvement is trustworthy by construction. Patches populate a gated, categorical quality-diversity archive (GSME) keyed on the (WHERE x WHY) pathology an edit addresses rather than the tasks it fixes, an anti-overfitting inductive bias; generalization is measured on a sealed test scored only after evolution. Across seven domains with a frozen open-weight model, the harness is train-selected and scored once on a sealed test; its credited gains there are +9 to +15.5pp and retain 86-147% of the training gain, evidence they generalize rather than overfit. The winning patch tracks the model's dominant pathology, not its size or family: changing the model can change the pathology and the patch, while the same pathology-to-patch match recurs across two model families. What transfers is the diagnose-and-credit loop, not any specific harness.

</details>


### [113] [The Test Oracle Problem in Synthetic LLM-as-Judge Corpora: Disappearance, Distortion and a Validation Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13707)
*Serkan Ballı*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个LLM作为评判者（LLM-as-judge）系统中的隐藏故障：生成幻觉答案时的解码预算参数截断了输出，导致构建的多语言（土耳其语/英语） faithfulness 语料库出现32个百分点的跨语言崩溃假象。该效应被三层机制解释、从N=50复制到N=500、通过交换实验验证——但全为虚假。故障仅在手动阅读原始生成时才被发现，任何聚合统计检查均无法察觉。另一偏差（Markdown格式偏好）也被同一故障扭曲，其幅度和符号随刺激长度变化。作者将问题框架化为测试预言问题：LLM生成的负例缺乏机械验证方式，而确定性扰动词典则自带项目级预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM-as-judge评估语料库构建中一个结构性的、而非偶然的故障模式：当负例（幻觉答案）由LLM生成时，解码预算等共享参数会悄无声息地破坏数据完整性，导致虚假但统计上显著的效应无法被常规统计检测发现。

Method: 通过一个多语言faithfulness评判语料库的案例分析，发现生成与评判阶段共享的解码预算参数截断了幻觉答案；采用三层机制分析、N=50到N=500复制实验、生产者交换实验来展示虚假效应；通过修正参数后效应消失来确认故障；引入测试预言问题框架，并与确定性扰动语料库进行正面对比控制实验。

Result: 共享参数导致的故障产生了32个百分点的跨语言collapse虚假效应；该效应可被三层机制解释、可复制、可通过交换实验验证，但完全不真实；另一个偏差（Markdown偏好）的幅度和符号也被同一故障扭曲；确定性扰动语料库可通过零成本字符串比较100%捕获类似故障。

Conclusion: LLM生成的负例语料库面临测试预言问题（无法机械验证项目完整性），而确定性扰动语料库自带项目级预言；作者提出针对无预言机制的验证协议，强调手动阅读原始生成的必要性。

Abstract: Studies of bias in LLM-as-judge systems typically build synthetic corpora by prompting an LLM to generate a hallucinated answer to pair with a factual one, then presenting both to a judge. We report a case in which this generation step silently failed, and use it to argue that the failure mode is structural rather than incidental. In a multilingual (Turkish/English) faithfulness-judgment corpus, a decoding-budget parameter shared between judging and generation calls truncated one producer's hallucinated answers to a few words. The resulting items produced a large, statistically robust effect: a 32-point cross-lingual collapse in one judge's selection accuracy, replicated from N=50 to N=500, explained by a three-layer mechanistic account, and confirmed by a controlled producer-swap experiment, none of which was real. The effect vanished to ceiling once the shared parameter was corrected, and only manual reading of the raw generations, not any aggregate statistical check, exposed the fault. A second measured bias (Markdown-formatting preference) was not fabricated but distorted by the same fault, its magnitude and in one case its sign shifting with stimulus length, a mode aggregate metrics cannot distinguish from the first. We frame the underlying vulnerability using the test oracle problem: corpora whose negative examples are LLM-generated carry no mechanical way to verify item integrity, while corpora built by deterministic perturbation of a gold answer carry an item-level oracle for free. A positive control supports this claim directly: an analogous fault injected into a minimal perturbation-based corpus is caught with 100% accuracy by a zero-cost, zero-human gold-to-negative string comparison. We close with a validation protocol, derived from our own case, for analysts working in the oracle-less regime that we argue describes most contemporary multilingual LLM-as-judge corpora.

</details>


### [114] [Self-supervised Speech Comparison for L2 Phone, Rhythm, and Intonation Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13721)
*Stephen McIntosh,Reuben Smit,Daisuke Saito,Nobuaki Minematsu,Herman Kamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自监督WavLM表示的DTW框架，无需文本转录即可评估L2语音的发音准确性、节奏和语调。在英语和日语L2语音上，基础DTW方法在音段层面的评分一致性超过人类；节奏评估方法接近人类水平；语调评估在某些任务上表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统L2语音评估侧重于音段层面的语音评分，对节奏、语调等超音段特征的评估研究不足；同时现有方法依赖标注L2语音数据，难以应用于低资源场景。

Method: 使用自监督WavLM表示，通过DTW（动态时间规整）将学习者语音与母语者模板进行对比。对于节奏，引入基于DTW对齐路径中规整程度的测量方法；对于语调，结合DTW距离与基于韵律残差、音高和强度特征的方法。

Result: 基础DTW方法在整体和句子级别的音段评分上超过人类一致性；节奏评估方法接近人类水平；语调评估在某些任务上表现较为有限。

Conclusion: 自监督表示结合DTW是一种有前景的、无需文本的、面向多维度发音评估的框架。

Abstract: L2 speech assessment has traditionally focused on phonetic assessment, leaving the scoring of suprasegmental features such as rhythm and intonation underexplored. Moreover, assessment methods often require training with labeled L2 speech data, making them difficult to apply in low-resource settings. We investigate whether DTW over self-supervised WavLM representations can provide a text-free framework for assessing phonetic accuracy, rhythm, and intonation in English and Japanese L2 speech. Results show that a basic DTW-based approach that compares learner speech to native templates exceeds human agreement on holistic and sentence-level phonetic scoring. For rhythm, we introduce methods that measure the degree of warping in the DTW alignment path; our best method approaches human-level performance. For intonation, we combine DTW distance over prosodic residuals with pitch and intensity features, but performance remains more modest on some tasks. Our results point to self-supervised representations as a promising, text-free basis for multi-aspect pronunciation assessment.

</details>


### [115] [Post-Training Shifts Confidence: A Three-Stage Analysis of How SFT, RL, and OPD Shape Pre-, Intra-, and Post-CoT Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13753)
*Shuhao Li,Guodong Du,Anhao Zhao,Wanyu Lin,Tianyu Yuan,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段校准框架，系统评估后训练方法（SFT、RL、OPD）在推理过程中不同阶段的置信度可靠性，发现置信度具有位置依赖性，并据此提出位置感知置信度策略PosConf，显著提升答案聚合和早停性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（SFT、RL、OPD）的评估仅关注最终答案准确率，忽略了对推理过程中置信度变化的影响，缺乏对模型置信度可靠性的系统性理解。

Method: 提出三阶段校准框架，分别评估推理前（难度估计）、推理中（早停）和推理后（答案聚合）的置信度。通过控制变量比较SFT、RL、OPD三种后训练方法，发现置信度的位置依赖性，并提出PosConf策略——仅在可靠相对位置区间使用置信度进行决策。

Result: OPD提供最优推理前置信度；SFT提供最强在线早停信号；RL产生最可靠的推理轨迹级置信度。PosConf在答案聚合上比多数投票提升6.1个点，在紧凑token预算下早停性能提升4.3个点。

Conclusion: 推理模型的置信度应分阶段、分位置使用，位置感知的置信度策略能有效规避后期逆校准问题，显著提升后训练方法的实用效果。

Abstract: Large language models have made strong reasoning gains through supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and on-policy distillation, yet these post-training methods are usually evaluated only by final-answer accuracy. We study how they reshape confidence during reasoning. We introduce a three-stage calibration framework that evaluates confidence before, during, and after chain-of-thought generation, corresponding to difficulty estimation, early termination, and answer aggregation. Through a controlled comparison on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we find that OPD provides the most useful pre-reasoning confidence, SFT gives the strongest online signal for early stopping, and RL produces the most reliable trace-level signal for aggregation. We further show that confidence reliability is position-dependent: RL confidence becomes informative after a path-commitment phase, while OPD confidence is useful early but can become inversely calibrated later. Based on this observation, we propose PosConf, a position-aware confidence strategy that uses confidence only from reliable relative-position intervals. PosConf improves RL answer aggregation by 6.1 points over majority voting and consistently improves OPD early stopping under tight token budgets, with gains up to 4.3 points by avoiding its later inverse-calibration region, showing that \emph{confidence in reasoning models should be used both stage-wise and position-awarely}. Our code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/Post-Training-Calibration.

</details>


### [116] [SPyCE: Skill-Policy Co-evolution for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13854)
*Ru Zhang,Weijie Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SPyCE框架，通过分层技能库与策略协同进化，将多模态推理轨迹蒸馏为可复用技能，在强化学习中实现闭环优化，在八个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法将轨迹简化为标量奖励，迫使策略从零开始发现工具使用模式；记忆方法依赖测试时检索而不更新策略。两者均未能将多模态推理轨迹中的可复用模式吸收进策略中。

Method: 提出SPyCE框架，构建分层技能库（执行技能捕获局部视觉操作，工作流技能编码工具使用的高层先验），在强化学习中策略依据检索到的技能生成轨迹，技能库则从有价值的轨迹中进化更新，形成闭环协同进化。

Result: 在八个基准上，SPyCE一致优于基于RL和基于记忆的基线方法。消融实验证实分层技能设计和协同进化机制均至关重要。

Conclusion: 技能-策略联合优化是构建强大多模态智能体的有前景范式。

Abstract: Multimodal agents that think with images iteratively manipulate visual evidence and invoke tools across many steps. Existing reinforcement learning methods reduce trajectories to scalar rewards, forcing the policy to discover reusable tool-use patterns from scratch on every new task; memory-based alternatives retain past experience, yet they rely on test-time retrieval, without updating the policy to absorb reusable patterns from that experience. Our key insight is that multimodal reasoning trajectories should be distilled into reusable skills that co-evolve with the policy during training, rather than being consumed as rewards or retrieved from a static store. To this end, we propose SPyCE (Skill-Policy Co-evolution), a framework that distills trajectories into a hierarchical skill library and updates it throughout reinforcement learning. Execution skills capture local visual operations, while workflow skills encode high-level priors that orchestrate tool use. During training, the policy model conditions on retrieved skills to guide its rollouts, while the skill library evolves using valuable rollouts generated by the policy. This creates a closed loop in which improved policies yield better skills, and the evolving skill library, in turn, provides stronger priors for policy rollouts. Experiments across eight benchmarks demonstrate that SPyCE consistently outperforms both RL-based and memory-based baselines. Further analysis reveals that both the hierarchical skill design and the co-evolution mechanism are critical to our design. These results suggest joint skill-policy optimization as a promising paradigm for building capable multimodal agents.

</details>


### [117] [High-Order Question Generation in a Multilingual Educational Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13901)
*Suna-Şeyma Uçar,Itziar Aldabe,Nora Aranberri,Orphée De Clercq*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨利用大语言模型（LLMs）基于Bloom分类法之外的替代框架（Claim-Evidence-Reasoning和Divergent Questioning）在巴斯克语、西班牙语和英语三种语言环境下生成高阶问题。结果表明，开源和商业模型均能有效生成问题，但仅约一半的可回答问题被教师认定为高阶问题；替代框架能产生结构性和概念性多样的问题，可作为Bloom分类法的可行补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批判性思维是超越简单记忆的关键技能，高阶提问是培养该技能的重要途径，但教师制作高阶问题存在困难，课堂实践中多依赖低阶问题。现有研究局限于Bloom分类法且仅关注英语，缺乏多语言和替代框架的探索。

Method: 基于Claim-Evidence-Reasoning和Divergent Questioning两种替代框架构建提示词，在巴斯克语、西班牙语和英语三种语言环境下，使用开源和商业大语言模型生成高阶问题，并由教师评估问题质量。

Result: 两种模型在三种语言下均能有效生成问题；但仅约50%的可回答问题被教师认定为高阶；替代框架能产生结构性和概念性多样的问题，表明它们可以相互补充。

Conclusion: 替代框架（CER和DQ）可作为Bloom分类法的可行替代方案，在自动生成高阶问题方面有互补价值；但仍需改进模型生成问题的教师认可度。

Abstract: Critical thinking is a fundamental skill that helps learners move beyond simple memorization. One way to develop this skill is through high-order questioning. However, crafting such questions remains a challenge for educators, and classroom practices tend to rely on low-order questions. Large Language Models have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating high-order questions, especially when guided by prompts based on Bloom's Taxonomy. Yet, existing research has largely centered on this framework and focused only on English. This study addresses these gaps by introducing prompts grounded in two alternative frameworks: Claim-Evidence-Reasoning and Divergent Questioning within a multilingual context using Basque, Spanish, and English. Results indicate that while both an open-source and a proprietary model rather effectively generate questions in all three languages, only about half of the answerable questions are recognized by teachers as high-order. A positive finding is that the alternative frameworks produce structurally and conceptually varied questions, suggesting they could complement each other and provide viable alternatives to Bloom's Taxonomy.

</details>


### [118] [DeepStress: Stress-Testing Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13920)
*Ismael Rousseau,Geraldine Damnati,Frederic Bechet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DeepStress压力测试框架，通过控制检索证据的可信度、相关性和事实性三个维度，系统评估搜索代理在处理不可靠信息时的鲁棒性。实验表明不同代理对低质量证据的应对能力存在显著差异，并提出了更优的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索代理在多步问答中表现出色，但其对低质量证据的鲁棒性未被充分探索。现实基准测试中低质量证据罕见，但在真实应用中可能导致严重失败，因此亟需系统性评估。

Method: 提出DeepStress框架，用受控合成环境替换搜索代理的检索模块，控制证据在可信度、相关性和事实性三个维度的质量分布。在HotpotQA和BrowseCompPlus数据集上测试多种搜索代理。

Result: 不同搜索代理在应对不可靠信息时表现出显著的能力差异，现有评估指标不足以全面反映系统表现。

Conclusion: DeepStress能有效揭示代理在低质量证据下的脆弱性，提出的新指标能更好刻画系统行为及参数知识与检索知识间的冲突交互。

Abstract: While search agents demonstrate impressive capabilities in multi-step question answering, their robustness to poor-quality evidence remains under-explored. This phenomenon occurs rarely in realistic benchmarks but can lead to dramatic failure in real life applications. Therefore in this study we propose DeepStress, a stress testing framework that controls the frequency of challenging evidence by replacing the retrieval module of search agents with a controlled synthetic environment. We use this framework to control three dimensions that can affect document reliability: trustworthiness, relevance, and factuality. Testing several search agents on HotpotQA and BrowseCompPlus, we demonstrate that agents exhibit substantial differences in their ability to handle unreliable information and propose new metrics that better document systems outcomes as well as the interactions between conflicting parametric and retrieved knowledge.

</details>


### [119] [DeltaMerge-LowRes: Composing Language and Task Deltas for Low-Resource Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13967)
*Son Ha Xuan,Xuan-Bach Le,Phat T. Tran-Truong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将多语言编码器对新语言的适应（语言增量）和新任务的适应（任务增量）分离学习并在权重空间中重组，提出了跨轴TIES等四种组合规则，在4种非洲语言的4个任务家族上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的低资源NLP设置中，新语言适应和新任务学习通常通过昂贵的语言-任务联合微调完成。作者探究是否可以将两者分离学习后，在权重空间中组合，从而节省计算资源并提高灵活性。

Method: 提出ΔMergeLowRes方法：从无标注单语文本学习语言增量Δ_L，从有标签英语数据学习任务增量Δ_T，推理时通过四种规则组合：加法、激活引导、稀疏感知，以及新提出的跨轴TIES（将TIES-Merging的修剪、符号选举、合并步骤应用于语言轴和任务轴而非两个任务轴）。

Result: 跨轴TIES在3/4种语言的摘要任务上提升了+4到+7 chrF（chrF 18.59 vs 任务仅微调13.80）；在QA任务上F1提升+2.32，EM提升+2.91；稀疏感知合并将分类任务的ECE降低36%且macro-F1持平。

Conclusion: 组合规则对合并模型保留什么、抑制什么以及校准情况有实质性影响。跨轴TIES在摘要和QA任务上表现最佳，稀疏感知合并提升分类校准。研究表明语言适应和任务适应可以分离学习后有效组合。

Abstract: Adapting a multilingual encoder to a new language \emph{and} a new task with only a few hundred gold examples is a common low-resource NLP setting, yet the two axes are usually fused via an expensive language--task fine-tuning run. We ask whether they can instead be trained separately and recombined in weight space. \DeltaMergeLowRes{} learns a language delta $Δ_L$ from unlabeled monolingual text and a task delta $Δ_T$ from labeled English data, then composes them at inference under one of four rules: additive, activation-guided, sparsity-aware, and a novel \emph{cross-axis TIES}. The new rule adapts the TIES-Merging steps of trimming, sign election, and merging to the language and task axes rather than to two task axes. Holding $(Δ_L,Δ_T)$ fixed across rules on four task families and four African languages ($158$ evaluated cells, $10{,}000$-sample paired bootstrap per cell), we find: (i) cross-axis TIES wins summarisation on $3/4$ languages by $+4$ to $+7$ chrF (chrF $18.59$ vs.\ $13.80$ task-only); (ii) it improves QA F1 by $+2.32$ and EM by $+2.91$; and (iii) sparsity-aware merging cuts classification ECE by $36\%$ at parity macro-F1. The composition rule materially changes what the merged model preserves, suppresses, and calibrates. We release all JSON traces and a claim ledger.

</details>


### [120] [Constraint-Aware Counterfactual Editing for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13977)
*S M Rafiuddin,Vamsi Krishna Pavuluri,Atriya Sen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAVE-ABSA 提出了一种约束感知的验证编辑框架，用于生成和验证面向方面的反事实样本，确保仅翻转目标方面的情感，同时保持非目标方面情感、语义、流畅性和事实一致性不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实生成方法多聚焦于句子级标签翻转，生成的编辑虽流畅但常存在方面无效、语义漂移或矛盾等问题，无法满足方面级情感分析（ABSA）对反事实样本的严格要求。

Method: CAVE-ABSA 包含五个关键步骤：定位目标方面的意见跨度、受控反事实重写、修复模块优化候选样本、通过方面级验证、语义相似度、AMR结构保留、编辑最小性、流畅性和矛盾检测等多维度过滤。

Result: 生成经过验证的方面级反事实数据集，可用于鲁棒性评估和数据增强，确保反事实样本在情感翻转上的有效性和语言质量。

Conclusion: 通过将生成与验证分离，CAVE-ABSA 提供了构建有意义的方面局部反事实样本的原则性方法，并能检验ABSA模型是否真正依赖基于方面的情感推理。

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) requires models to identify sentiment toward specific aspects rather than relying on the global polarity of a sentence. This makes counterfactual evaluation especially challenging: a valid counterfactual should flip the sentiment of one target aspect while preserving the sentiment of all non-target aspects, semantic meaning, fluency, and factual consistency. Existing counterfactual generation methods often focus on sentence-level label flipping and may produce edits that are fluent but aspect-invalid, semantically drifting, or contradictory. To address this limitation, we propose CAVE-ABSA, a Constraint-Aware Validated Editing framework for generating and validating aspect-level counterfactuals. CAVE-ABSA localizes the opinion span associated with the target aspect, performs controlled counterfactual rewriting, refines candidates through a repair module, and filters them using aspect-level verification, semantic similarity, AMR-guided structural preservation, edit minimality, fluency, and contradiction detection. The framework is designed to construct validated counterfactual ABSA datasets for robustness evaluation and data augmentation. By explicitly separating generation from validation, CAVE-ABSA provides a principled approach for producing meaningful aspect-local counterfactuals and for testing whether ABSA models truly rely on aspect-grounded sentiment reasoning.

</details>


### [121] [Can an Old Dog Be Taught New Tricks? Taking LLMs Beyond Sentence Level Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14040)
*Alaina Brandt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAT系统，一种基于RAG的整文档翻译方法，利用用户配置和可比语料库将段落、章节和文档级示例传递给LLM进行整文档生成。实验表明LLM可以超越逐句翻译范式实现语篇重构，但重构效果仍有待改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动翻译系统（从CAT工具到机器翻译）几乎都基于逐句翻译范式，忽略了语篇组织、修辞风格和语用规范等跨语言差异。作者希望探索LLM能否突破这一局限，实现整文档的、基于语料库信息的翻译。

Method: 构建PAT系统，采用RAG架构：用户配置规范 + 可比语料库（美国英语与拉美西班牙语长篇文本），检索段落、章节和文档级示例供LLM进行整文档生成。使用自定义MQM类型学评估，由两名训练有素的评估员对三个项目的六种自动翻译进行评测。

Result: 有限提示词未产生有意义的语篇重构；包含规范和语料库信息的翻译在某些情况下表现出实质性重构，但并非总是有效。表明LLM能够向语篇重构方向移动，但重构效果仍需改进。

Conclusion: LLM可以超越逐句翻译范式，但需要更多工作来提高语篇重构的有效性。论文还讨论了自动翻译系统设计、语料库构建和翻译质量评估方法学等方面的考量。

Abstract: Automatic translation systems, from CAT tools to MT, overwhelmingly treat translation as a sentence-by-sentence act. This paper asks whether LLMs can be moved beyond that paradigm through whole-document, corpus-informed translation. We present PAT (Pragmatic Auto-Translator), a RAG-based system that pairs user-configured specifications with context from a comparable corpus of authentic longform texts in U.S. English and Latin American Spanish, passing retrieved paragraph-, section-, and document-level examples to an LLM for whole-document generation. The goal is draft translation for professional verification: target texts reformulated to fit their Spanish-language context, where discourse organization, rhetorical style, and pragmatic norms differ meaningfully from English. We evaluated six automatic translations of essays on generative AI across three projects using a customized MQM typology, assessed by two trained evaluators working from U.S. English into LATAM and Mexican Spanish. Results show that a limited prompt produced no meaningful reformulation, and specifications and corpus-informed translations at times showed substantial reformulation, though not always to effect. We find that LLMs can be moved toward reformulation and away from the sentence-by-sentence paradigm, though more work is needed to improve the effectiveness of those reformulations. In this paper, we discuss considerations related to automatic translation system design, corpus construction, and translation quality evaluation methodology and results.

</details>


### [122] [Hindcast: Replaying Prediction Markets to Evaluate LLM Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14051)
*Xiao Ye,Jacob Dineen,Evan Zhu,Shijie Lu,Kevin Song,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Hindcast评测框架，通过冻结Reddit快照和设定历史时间点t₀，消除LLM预测评测中因信息泄露（检索后见报道和训练数据包含未来事件）导致的回溯性偏差，使评测真正衡量预测能力而非记忆力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测评测存在两重信息泄露：一是检索系统可能返回事件发生后撰写的报道，将预测任务退化为查表；二是新模型训练数据包含更接近事件发生时间的数据，使得原本未来的问题存在于训练集中。因此评测结果反映的是记忆能力而非真正的预测能力。

Method: Hindcast框架：1) 对每个Polymarket预测市场，设定历史截止时间t₀；2) 使用t₀之前发布的Reddit帖子作为模型可访问的静态知识库；3) 模型仅能读取t₀前的公开信息；4) 将模型预测同时与真实结果和市场在t₀时的价格（作为人类基准）对比；5) 知识库冻结不变，可随新市场持续复用。

Result: 在消除信息泄露后，检索增强对大多数模型仍有帮助，但仅限于Reddit在事件发生前已有相关讨论的场景；当档案中只有推测性内容时，检索反而损害预测性能。

Conclusion: Hindcast通过严格的时间隔离和静态知识库设计，实现了对LLM预测能力的无偏评测，揭示了检索增强在不同信息环境下的差异化影响，为预测评测提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Forecasters are evaluated by backtesting, which replays resolved questions and grades the probability the system would have assigned before the outcome was known. For LLMs, two channels leak the answer into this test. A model that retrieves can surface reports written after the event, turning forecasting into a lookup, and each new model is trained on data closer to the event, so a question that lay in the future for last year's models sits inside this year's training data. Either way, the test grades recall while claiming to grade foresight. We introduce Hindcast, which closes both leaks by grading a model as if it stood at a chosen past date $t_0$, before the outcome existed in either channel. Hindcast replays resolved Polymarket prediction markets against a frozen snapshot of public Reddit, lets the model read only posts written before $t_0$, and scores each forecast against both what happened and the market's own price at $t_0$, itself a human forecast made from the same past information. Because the cutoff is set per market and the snapshot never changes, the evaluation re-runs on new markets as models improve, without going stale. Once the leak is closed, retrieval still helps most models, but only where Reddit discussed the event beforehand. Where the archive carried only speculation, retrieval hurts.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Mixed-Timescale Differential Coding for Downlink Model Broadcast in Wireless Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13119)
*Chung-Hsuan Hu,Zheng Chen,Erik G. Larsson*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对联邦学习中全局模型广播因链路失败导致设备错过差分更新而无法重建模型的问题，提出混合时间尺度差分编码（MTDC）方案，通过调整参考模型在两个层级进行差分编码，使设备能在两次全模型广播之间重建最新全局模型，并设计了年龄感知变体和设备调度策略，仿真表明在相似通信资源下优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中利用时序相关性对全局模型进行差分编码可减少通信量，但无线链路失败会导致设备错过差分更新，进而使用过时模型或空闲等待，影响学习性能。现有方法缺乏对这类丢包场景下模型重建的有效机制。

Method: 提出混合时间尺度差分编码（MTDC）方案，通过调整参考模型在两个不同时间尺度上执行差分编码；基于收敛分析设计年龄感知变体（age-aware MTDC）以及相应的设备调度策略，以进一步提升通信效率。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在相似通信资源预算且存在下行链路传输失败的情况下，所提MTDC方案相较于基线方法取得了更优的学习性能。

Conclusion: MTDC能有效缓解下行链路失败对联邦学习的影响，通过双时间尺度差分编码和年龄感知调度实现了鲁棒且高效的全局模型传播。

Abstract: In standard federated learning systems, the parameter server broadcasts the global model to the participating devices in every iteration. Motivated by the temporal correlation between consecutive global models, differential coding can be applied to global model dissemination to reduce the information magnitude, thereby enabling communication with fewer quantization bits. However, due to wireless link failures, devices may occasionally miss differential updates and consequently fail to reconstruct the global model. As a result, they either continue local training based on an outdated model or remain idle until the next full-model broadcast becomes available. To address this challenge, we propose a mixed-timescale differential coding (MTDC) scheme that performs differential coding at two different levels by adjusting the reference model. With MTDC, a device can reconstruct the latest global model between two full-model broadcasts even if it misses a differential update. We provide a convergence analysis that motivates the design of an age-aware variant of MTDC, along with a device scheduling policy to further improve communication efficiency. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed MTDC schemes achieve superior learning performance compared to baseline methods under similar communication resource budgets in the presence of downlink transmission failures.

</details>


### [124] [Active Beyond-Diagonal RIS Empowered Heterogeneous Edge Computing: A Distributional Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13160)
*Tianyu Pang,Hongyu Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对基于互易有源BD-RIS的异构MEC系统，提出了一种DSAC-T端到端联合优化框架，解决混合整数非凸问题，实现能效与延迟的最优权衡，达到81.67%可行性率和0.0267s在线决策时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互易有源BD-RIS在实际中由互易器件实现，会产生跨扇区能量泄漏，重塑系统级能量-延迟权衡。现有方法难以高效求解紧耦合的卸载决策、计算分配、发射功率、接收处理和BD-RIS配置所形成的高维混合整数非凸问题。

Method: 提出基于改进的分布式软演员-评论家算法（DSAC-T）的端到端联合优化框架，通过建模回报分布而非仅期望值，在奖励异质性和可行性边界敏感性下提升策略稳定性。

Result: DSAC-T在能量-延迟奖励上达到最优，可行性比率为81.67%，在线决策时间为0.0267秒/场景。

Conclusion: DSAC-T在解决有源BD-RIS辅助异构MEC中的高维混合整数非凸问题上优于基线算法，验证了回报分布建模对策略稳定性的提升作用。

Abstract: Active beyond-diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (BD-RISs) enables hybrid transmitting and reflecting mode to achieve effective signal amplification and full-space coverage, thus providing a promising solution for blockage-aware uplink offloading in heterogeneous mobile edge computing (MEC) systems. However, practical hybrid mode active BD-RIS are realized by reciprocal devices, which inherently generate cross-sector energy leakage that will reshape the system-level energy-latency tradeoff. This paper studies energy-aware offloading and resource allocation for reciprocal active BD-RIS-assisted heterogeneous MEC, where offloading decisions, CPU/GPU computation allocation, transmit powers, receive processing, and active BD-RIS are tightly coupled. The resulting problem is a high-dimensional mixed integer nonconvex problem and is difficult to solve efficiently by conventional per-instance optimization. To address this challenge, we develop an end-to-end joint optimization framework based on a refined version of the distributional soft actor--critic algorithm, named as DSAC-T. By modeling return distributions rather than only expected values, DSAC-T improves policy stability under reward heterogeneity and feasibility-boundary sensitivity. Compared with other baseline algorithms, DSAC-T achieves the best energy-latency reward, the highest feasibility ratio of 81.67%, and a fast online decision time of 0.0267 s per scenario.

</details>


### [125] [Construction of Generalized Weighing-Hadamard Matrices over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13324)
*Gustavo T. Bastos,Miguel Beltrá,Sara D. Cardell,Verónica Requena*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上的广义加权Hadamard（GWH）矩阵的存在性、性质与构造。可逆GWH矩阵构成乘法群；通过正交变换定义强等价关系，并证明相应的商群是阿贝尔群；最后讨论了在编码理论中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义加权Hadamard矩阵是经典Hadamard矩阵在有限域上的推广，具有重要的代数结构与编码理论应用潜力，但目前对其群论性质与等价分类的研究尚不充分。

Method: 通过群论方法分析可逆GWH矩阵的乘法封闭性；引入正交变换定义强等价关系，进而构造商群并分析其交换性。

Result: 证明了可逆GWH矩阵构成乘法群；在正交变换等价意义下，商群是阿贝尔群。

Conclusion: GWH矩阵具有丰富的代数结构，为编码理论提供了新的设计工具。

Abstract: The existence, several properties, and constructions of Generalized Weighing-Hadamard (GWH) matrices over finite fields are addressed in this work. We study the subset of invertible GWH matrices and show that it forms a group under matrix multiplication. Besides that, we introduce a strong notion of equivalence between such matrices, defined via orthogonal transformations, and further prove that the corresponding quotient group by the subgroup of orthogonal matrices is abelian. Finally, we discuss some applications of these matrices in coding theory

</details>


### [126] [$r$-Minimal Poset Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13520)
*Yang Xu,Haibin Kan,Guangyue Han*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了基于偏序集 $\mathbf{P}$-支撑的 $r$-最小码，将其与切割 $r$-阻塞映射、$(\mathbf{P},\omega)$-权重等概念建立等价刻画，推广了汉明度量下最小码的经典理论，并在分层偏序集等特殊情形下给出了存在性结果与性质刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将汉明度量下最小码的概念推广到偏序集支撑的场景，提出 $r$-最小 $\mathbf{P}$-码这一更一般的编码框架，以统一和扩展已有研究成果。

Method: 引入切割 $r$-阻塞映射的概念来刻画 $r$-最小 $\mathbf{P}$-码；利用 $(\mathbf{P},\omega)$-权重函数给出 $r$-最小性的充要条件，推广Ashikhmin-Barg准则；通过存在性证明（一般偏序集与不相交链的并）及分层偏序集下的约化分析，建立与汉明度量 $r$-最小码的联系。

Result: (1) 给出了 $r$-最小 $\mathbf{P}$-码的切割 $r$-阻塞映射刻画；(2) 给出了基于 $(\mathbf{P},\omega)$-权重的充要条件，推广了Ashikhmin-Barg准则；(3) 证明了 $r$-最小 $\mathbf{P}$-码的存在性；(4) 在分层偏序集情形下建立了与汉明度量 $r$-最小码的等价关系；(5) 刻画了两层分层偏序集诱导的切割 $r$-阻塞集，回答了Hyun等人的公开问题。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了 $r$-最小 $\mathbf{P}$-码的理论框架，在偏序集编码与最小码之间架起了桥梁，并为后续研究提供了新的工具与开放问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study $r$-minimal codes with respect to $\mathbf{P}$-support, where $\mathbf{P}=(Ω,\preccurlyeq_{\mathbf{P}})$ is a poset defined on the coordinate set of the ambient space $\mathbf{H}$. $r$-Minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes are natural extensions of Hamming metric minimal codes that have been extensively studied in the literature. We characterize $r$-minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes in terms of the notion so called cutting $r$-blocking maps, which generalizes the well-known equivalence between minimal Hamming metric codes and cutting blocking sets. We also give a necessary and sufficient condition for $r$-minimality in terms of $(\mathbf{P},ω)$-weight defined on $\mathbf{H}$, where $ω:Ω\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}$ is an arbitrary weight function. This leads to a generalization of the well-known Ashikhmin-Barg criterion for Hamming metric minimal codes. We then prove two existence results for $r$-minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes, both for general $\mathbf{P}$ and for the special case that $\mathbf{P}$ is a disjoint union of chains. When $\mathbf{P}$ is hierarchical, we characterize $r$-minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes in terms of $r$-minimal Hamming metric codes. Finally, we characterize cutting $r$-blocking sets induced by hierarchical posets with two levels, which further enables us to answer a question raised in Hyun, Kim, Wu and Yue \cite{28}.

</details>


### [127] [Optimal Non-Binary Single-Track Gray Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13588)
*Tuvi Etzion*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究单轨格雷码（single-track Gray code）的存在性。对于素数p=3和p=5，证明了长度为p^t、包含p^{p^t}个码字、字母表为F_p的单轨格雷码存在；对于更大的素数p，t=2时存在则对任意t>2也存在；非素数字母表下也有存在性指示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轨格雷码在量化和编码应用中相比传统格雷码有优势，但二进制情况下（除n=2外）无法包含全部2^n个码字，因此需要探索其他字母表大小下的构造可能性。

Method: 通过代数构造方法，在有限域F_p（p为素数）上构造长度为p^t、码字数为p^{p^t}的单轨格雷码，并研究其存在条件与递推关系。

Result: 对p=3和p=5证明了此类代码的存在性；对更大的素数p，若t=2时存在则可推广至任意t>2；对非素数字母表也有存在性迹象。

Conclusion: 单轨格雷码在素数幂长度的有限域上存在，尤其是小素数情形完全解决，大素数和非素数情形也有进展。

Abstract: A single-track Gray code is a cyclic Gray code with codewords of length $n$, over an alphabet of size $m$, such that all the $n$ tracks that correspond to the $n$ distinct coordinates of the codewords are cyclic shifts of the first track. Such codes have advantages over the conventional Gray codes in certain quantization and coding applications. Unless $n=2$, there are no such binary codes that contain all the $2^n$ codewords of length $n$. In this paper, we prove that such codes of length $p^t$, $n \geq 2$, with $p^{p^t}$ codewords, over $\F_p$, $p$ prime, exist, for $p=3$ and $p=5$. For larger prime $p$ an appropriate code for $t=2$, implies the existence of such a code for any $t >2$. If the alphabet size $m$ is not a prime there are also indications that such codes exist.

</details>


### [128] [Low-Complexity Soft-Aided Error-and-Erasure Decoding for Generalized Product Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13719)
*Sisi Miao,Laurent Schmalen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RDRSD的硬消息传递迭代纠错与擦除译码器，用于广义乘积码（GPCs）。该译码器利用动态可靠性分数，在复杂度与性能之间提供灵活权衡，相比iBDD可提升约1dB编码增益，并针对小纠错能力分量码提出了软辅助后处理步骤以降低错误平层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义乘积码（GPCs）兼具优异的高码率性能和低复杂度硬件实现优势，但现有译码方法在性能与复杂度之间尚未达到理想平衡，需要更高效的译码方案。

Method: 提出了精炼动态可靠性分数译码器（RDRSD），采用硬消息传递的迭代纠错与擦除译码机制，基于动态可靠性分数在综合征域实现，复杂度与硬判决iBDD相当；针对小纠错能力分量码，引入软辅助后处理步骤来降低错误平层。

Result: RDRSD在不同GPCs和译码配置下提供了多种复杂度-性能权衡，相比iBDD实现了约1dB的编码增益；软辅助后处理步骤显著降低了错误平层。

Conclusion: RDRSD是一种有效的GPC译码方案，在保持低复杂度的同时显著提升了译码性能，并通过后处理进一步改善了小纠错能力场景下的错误平层问题。

Abstract: Generalized product codes (GPCs) combine excellent high-rate performance with low-complexity hardware implementations. We propose the refined dynamic reliability score decoder (\acs{RDRSD}), a hard-message-passing iterative error-and-erasure decoder that uses dynamic reliability scores. Its syndrome-domain implementation has complexity comparable to hard-decision \ac{iBDD}. Across various GPCs and decoding configurations, \acs{RDRSD} provides different complexity--performance trade-offs and achieves approximately \SI{1}{dB} coding gain over \ac{iBDD}. For GPCs with component codes of small error-correcting capability, we also analyze the error floor and propose a soft-aided post-processing step that significantly lowers it.

</details>


### [129] [Precoding-based protocols for entanglement assisted linear computation over a quantum many-to-one network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13756)
*Ruoyu Meng,Aditya Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在无噪声量子多对一网络中，多个发送方利用共享纠缠但不与接收方共享纠缠的情况下，计算线性组合的问题。作者提出新的协议支持更一般的线性变换，通过给发送方更多量子位并允许他们预编码输入符号，通信成本不超过已知最佳结果，在某些情况下严格更低，并证明了通信成本跨实例的次可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有N-Sum Box协议（Allaix et al., 2025）对线性组合和数据向量分布有限制，作者希望扩展到更一般的线性变换，并探索通信成本的优化与次可加性。

Method: 通过给发送方更多量子位（qudits）并允许他们在传输前对输入符号进行预编码（precoding），设计新协议支持更一般类的线性变换。

Result: 所提方案的通信成本至多等于已知最佳结果，在某些情况下严格更低；同时证明了通信成本具有次可加性，即联合计算两个线性函数的总成本低于分别计算它们的成本之和。

Conclusion: 通过增加发送方的量子资源并引入预编码策略，成功扩展了可计算的线性变换范围，降低了通信成本，并揭示了量子网络计算中成本次可加性的重要性质。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the problem of computing a linear combination over a noiseless quantum many-to-one network. There are $k$ senders, Alice$_1$, $\ldots$, Alice$_k$, and a single receiver, Bob. Each Alice$_i$ has a data vector $W_i \in \mathbb{F}^{m_i}$, where $\mathbb{F}$ is a finite field. Bob wants to compute the linear combination $Y = V_1 W_1 + V_2 W_2 + \cdots + V_k W_k \in \mathbb{F}^m$, where $V_i$ is an $m \times m_i$ matrix over $\mathbb{F}$. The senders transmit quantum states to Bob through a noiseless many-to-one quantum network, but they are not allowed to communicate with each other. The senders share entanglement among themselves, while Bob does not share this entanglement. They encode their classical information $W_i$, $i=1,\ldots,k$, into their local subsystems and transmit them to Bob so that he can recover $Y$ through a quantum measurement and subsequent post-processing. The N-Sum Box protocol proposed by Allaix et al. (2025) considers this problem under certain constraints on the linear combination and the distribution of the data vectors among the senders.
  We present protocols that support the computation of a more general class of linear transformations by giving the senders access to more qudits and allowing them to judiciously precode their input symbols. The communication cost of our schemes is at most that of the best-known prior results in this area and is strictly lower in certain cases. Finally, we demonstrate that the communication cost is subadditive across instances. Specifically, we identify two linear functions for which the total cost of computing them individually is strictly larger than the cost of computing them jointly.

</details>


### [130] [CAS I: A Geometric Coding Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13796)
*Romie Banerjee*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文在对称群框架下建立了经典编码定理的直接类比，证明对于可修正收缩的对称群，对称先验是通用下半可计算半测度，并建立了子群与二进制串子集之间的伽罗瓦连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典编码定理推广到对称群设定中，统一算法信息论与群论，为对称诱导的复杂度度量提供理论框架。

Method: 定义对称群上的对称先验（随机选择的对称以给定串为唯一不动点的概率），证明可修正收缩群条件下该先验为通用下半可计算半测度；建立子群与二进制串子集间的伽罗瓦连接，刻画闭点和极大闭子群。

Result: 对称先验在可修正收缩对称群下满足几何编码定理；伽罗瓦连接揭示了子群与二进制串间的结构对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文统一了算法信息论与群论，为计算算法统计学（CAS）系列研究奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper establishes a direct analogue of the classical Coding Theorem in the setting of symmetry groups. We consider computable bijections on the set of binary strings, called symmetries and define the symmetry prior of a string as the probability that a randomly chosen symmetry from a given group has the string as its unique fixed point. We show that for any fix-retractable symmetry group, a group admitting a computable section that selects an isolating symmetry for every string, the symmetry prior is a universal lower semi-computable semi-measure. In this case, the Geometric Coding Theorem holds.
  We also develop a Galois connection between subgroups of G and subsets of binary strings, characterizing closed points and maximal closed subgroups, and explore the join-semilattice of dense subgroups. Our results unify algorithmic information theory with group theory and provide a framework for studying symmetry-induced complexity measures.
  This paper is the first in a series on Computational Algorithmic Statistics (CAS).

</details>


### [131] [Safety-Aware Forward Detection in Networked ISAC for Low-Altitude UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13908)
*Jingli Li,Yiyan Ma,Wei Chen,Weijie Yuan,Qingqing Cheng,Tongyang Xu,Guoyu Ma,Mi Yang,Yunlong Lu,Wenwei Yue,Zhangdui Zhong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向安全感知的前向探测设计方案，利用多地面基站协同，在支持无人机下行通信与状态估计的同时，实现对前向感兴趣区域（ROI）内非合作目标的可靠检测。推导了状态估计CRLB与前向ROI漏检概率的标度律，并构建了联合优化导频占比、发射功率和波束方向的安全感知资源分配问题。仿真表明，相比无前向探测方案，平均漏检概率降低17.05%，碰撞风险相应降低，而状态估计CRLB仅增加14.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络化通感一体化研究主要关注通信增强或目标参数估计，对无人机飞行前向区域非合作目标的检测可靠性研究不足，存在安全隐患。

Method: 首先基于无人机位置、速度和安全制动距离确定前向ROI并体素化表征目标存在状态；推导CRLB与漏检概率的标度律；构建联合优化导频占比、发射功率和波束方向的资源分配问题。

Result: 相比对照方案，漏检概率降低17.05%，碰撞风险相应降低；状态估计CRLB仅增加14.82%，性能退化有限。

Conclusion: 所提安全感知前向探测设计在保证通信速率约束下，显著提升非合作目标检测可靠性，仅以轻微状态估计性能代价换取安全性增益。

Abstract: Networked integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) exploits cooperation among multiple ground base stations (GBSs) to support safe uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) flight in low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs). Existing studies mainly focus on communication enhancement or target parameter estimation, while the detection reliability of non-cooperative targets in the UAV forward region remains insufficiently investigated. To address this issue, this paper proposes a safety-aware forward detection design in networked ISAC, where multiple GBSs jointly support UAV downlink communication, state estimation, and non-cooperative target detection within the forward region of interest (ROI). First, the forward ROI is determined by the UAV position, velocity, and safe braking distance, and is voxelized to characterize target-existence states. Then, the Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRLB) for UAV state estimation and the forward-ROI miss-detection probability are derived, and their scaling laws are characterized: In detail, the UAV state-estimation CRLB approximately decreases as $\ln^{-2}J$ with the number of cooperative GBSs $J$, while the forward-ROI miss-detection probability follows an exponential-form scaling law as $λ_{t}D_{f}\ln^{-2}J$. Furthermore, a safety-aware resource optimization problem is formulated to jointly configure the sensing pilot ratio, transmit power, and beam direction, balancing UAV state-estimation performance and forward detection reliability under the communication-rate constraint. Simulation results show that, compared with the baseline scheme without forward detection, the proposed design reduces the average miss-detection probability and the corresponding sensing-induced collision risk by $17.05\%$, while introducing only limited state-estimation performance degradation, reflected by a $14.82\%$ increase in the average CRLB.

</details>


### [132] [Square-Root Law for Covert Communication with Warden-Favorable Side Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14013)
*Hossein Ahmadi,Christian Deppe,Boulat A. Bash,Eduard A. Jorswieck*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究标量高斯隐蔽叠加模型，在Willie拥有全部物理侧信息并能减去公共分量估计值的情况下，通过相对熵约束（预算δ）施加隐蔽性。稳态下，最大可靠隐蔽载荷为 R_C^*√n (1+o(1)) 比特，其中平方根律常数由Willie实际检测输入的方差决定，而非仅由接收机噪声决定。低功率高斯信号可达该常数，匹配逆推证明了条件加性高斯创新模型下的一阶最优性。对于时变条件残差方差，给出了相应的一阶功率分配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐蔽通信研究通常假设Willie仅受接收机噪声限制，未充分考虑其可利用的丰富侧信息（如协议细节、定时、导频、信道估计、校准信息等）以及由此产生的不可约抵消误差。本文旨在更实际的模型下刻画隐蔽通信的容量极限。

Method: 建立标量高斯隐蔽叠加模型，Alice的低功率隐蔽信号叠加在公共分量上，Willie减去其最佳估计后对残差进行检测。隐蔽性通过以Willie侧信息为条件的相对熵约束（预算δ）来度量。采用高斯创新假设，分析稳态和时变两种情况下的最大可靠隐蔽载荷，并推导匹配逆推。

Result: 稳态下最大可靠隐蔽载荷为 R_C^*√n (1+o(1)) 比特，其中 R_C^* = (σ_0^2)/(σ_B^2 ln 2) √δ，σ_0^2=σ_W^2+σ_e^2 为残差方差（接收机噪声与不可约抵消误差之和）。低功率高斯信号可达该常数。对于时变条件残差方差，给出了将更多隐蔽功率分配给较大残差方差的一阶分配方案。

Conclusion: 平方根律常数由Willie实际检测输入的方差（含不可约抵消误差）决定，而非仅由接收机噪声决定。低功率高斯信令在该模型下达到一阶最优。结果受限于高斯后抵消零残差且条件方差已知的假设，非高斯残差和固定非消失方差不确定性不在本文范围内。

Abstract: Covert communication enables Alice to transmit to Bob while making the transmission difficult for Willie to detect. We study a scalar Gaussian covert-overlay model in which Alice's low-power covert signal is superimposed on an aggregate public component generated by Alice or other trackable sources. Willie is given all physically obtainable side information, including protocol details, timing, pilots, channel estimates, and calibration information, and subtracts his best estimate of the public component before testing. Covertness is imposed on the resulting residual through a relative-entropy constraint with budget $δ$ conditioned on Willie's side information. In the stationary case, the residual under no covert transmission has variance $σ_0^2=σ_W^2+σ_e^2$, where $σ_W^2$ is Willie's receiver-noise variance and $σ_e^2$ is the irreducible cancellation error. Over $n$ channel uses, the maximal reliably transmissible covert payload is $R_C^\star\sqrt{n}(1+o(1))$ bits, where $R_C^\star=\frac{σ_0^2}{σ_B^2\ln 2}\sqrtδ$, and $σ_B^2$ is Bob's receiver-noise variance. Thus, the square-root-law (SRL) constant is governed by the variance at Willie's actual detector input, not by receiver noise alone. Low-power Gaussian signaling achieves this constant, and a matching converse establishes first-order optimality within the conditioned additive Gaussian innovation model. For known time-varying conditioned residual variances, we also derive the first-order allocation, which assigns more covert power to larger residual variances. The results require a Gaussian post-cancellation null residual with known conditioned variance; non-Gaussian residuals and fixed non-vanishing variance uncertainty are outside the scope of this paper.

</details>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Exploring the Alignment of Generation and Understanding in Protein Structure Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13503)
*Junde Xu,Yuansheng Huang,Zijun Gao,Lihang Liu,Xiaoming Fang,Yu Kang,Jiezhong Qiu,Pheng Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了蛋白质生成模型在理解任务上的表现差距，并提出通过表示对齐（REPA）框架将生成式蛋白质扩散模型与预训练的理解模型对齐，显著提升了功能蛋白生成质量（MotifBench分数提升20%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管理解和生成在训练目标上密切相关（如去噪和掩码预测），但现有研究表明视觉领域的生成模型学到的表征对理解任务次优。本文旨在探究蛋白质领域是否也存在类似的理解-生成差距，并寻找弥补该差距的方法。

Method: 系统性评估了现有蛋白质生成模型在理解任务上的表现，发现其明显劣于专用蛋白质编码器。受REPA框架启发，在训练过程中将蛋白质扩散模型显式与预训练的蛋白质理解模型进行表示对齐。

Result: 在MotifBench数据集上，表示对齐将Protpardelle-1c的分数从39.2提升至47.1，相对提升20%。

Conclusion: 表示对齐为桥接蛋白质结构建模中的理解与生成提供了一种通用且有效的机制。

Abstract: Understanding and generation are often treated as two separate paradigms in training deep neural networks, despite the fact that both are trained with closely related objectives such as denoising and masked prediction. While prior studies have shown that generative models often learn suboptimal representations for understanding tasks in vision, it is less understood whether a similar gap exists in the protein domain. In this work, we systematically investigate this question by benchmarking state-of-the-art protein generative models on widely-used protein understanding tasks, and observe that these models exhibit consistently poor performance compared to existing protein encoders. Furthermore, inspired by the Representation Alignment (REPA) framework, we propose to explicitly align generative protein diffusion models with pretrained protein understanding models during training. Experiments on the MotifBench demonstrate that representation alignment significantly improves functional protein generation, boosting the MotifBench score of Protpardelle-1c from 39.2 to 47.1, corresponding to a 20% relative improvement. Our results suggest that representation alignment provides a general and effective mechanism for bridging understanding and generation in protein structure modeling.

</details>


### [134] [On phase-field regularization in dynamic fracture with brittle and cohesive formulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13599)
*Jonas Heinzmann,Francesco Vicentini,Pietro Carrara,Laura De Lorenzis*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文分析了三种相场断裂模型（脆性模型、刚度+密度退化模型、黏聚断裂模型）在动态条件下的波-裂纹相互作用特性，并通过一维和二维数值实验揭示了不同正则化方法对动态断裂行为的影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂模型在模拟裂纹成核与扩展中被广泛使用，但动态条件下相场正则化的作用尚未被充分理解，尤其是损伤变量与位移场的耦合方式对结果影响显著。

Method: 分析三种相场模型：脆性模型（仅刚度退化）、刚度+密度退化模型、以及作者先前提出的黏聚断裂相场正则化模型（扩展至弹性动力学）。通过一维杆中拉伸与压缩弹性波与相场裂纹的相互作用研究，结合二维缺口板的动态分支行为分析。

Result: 确定了哪些模型在何种条件下能保留尖锐裂纹的波-裂纹相互作用特征；从理论上解释了控制行为的变量；为扩展至动力学的黏聚模型推导了分析型动态黏聚张开律。

Conclusion: 不同相场正则化方式对动态断裂模拟的保真度有本质影响，新提出的黏聚模型在动力学扩展中具有优越性，其分析型动态黏聚律为准确模拟提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Phase-field models of fracture are widely used for simulating crack nucleation and propagation, yet the role of the phase-field regularization in the dynamic regime is not fully understood and depends critically on how the damage variable is coupled to the displacement field. In this paper, we analyze three alternative formulations: the brittle model with stiffness degradation, its variant with stiffness+density degradation, and our recently proposed phase-field regularization of cohesive fracture, which we extend to elastodynamics. By studying the interaction of a tensile and a compressive elastic wave with a phase-field crack in a one-dimensional bar, we determine for which models and under which conditions the phase-field regularization preserves the features of the wave-crack interaction expected for a sharp crack, and we theoretically explain which variables control the behavior. For the new cohesive model extended to dynamics, we further derive an analytical dynamic cohesive opening law. Finally, we study the dynamic behavior including branching of a two-dimensional notched plate at two loading intensities.

</details>


### [135] [Accelerating gas-network feasibility screening with a physics-informed graph neural network surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13610)
*Dongrui Jiang,Jochen Garcke,Okan Akca,Jeremias Hollnagel,Bernhard Klaassen,Mehrnaz Anvari,Joachim Müller-Kirchenbauer*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息图神经网络代理模型，用于稳态天然气网仿真与可行性筛选。采用边中心架构预测管道压差和流量，通过可微投影层强制节点质量守恒，并利用拉普拉斯重构确保节点压力拓扑一致性。在GasLib-134/135/582基准上评估，582节点网络在5000场景训练后，压力MAE为1.05 bar（压力范围的1.3%），R²=0.981；流量投影预测R²=0.972，质量平衡残差降至10⁻⁵–10⁻⁴ Nm³/s量级。推理时间从秒级降至毫秒级（最大基准<40 ms）。该框架可作为高容量场景筛选与优先级排序的物理约束规划加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模气网场景评估是综合能源系统规划的算力瓶颈，传统非线性水力学求解器可靠但成本高昂，而基于学习的无约束代理模型可能产生水力不可行状态。因此需要开发一种兼具物理约束与计算效率的代理模型。

Method: 采用物理信息图神经网络（PGNN）框架：边中心架构预测管道级压差平方和流量；可微投影层强制流量满足节点质量守恒；拉普拉斯重构将边压差映射为拓扑一致的节点压力。通过无监督/混合训练策略，物理损失（质量守恒、环路压降）与监督损失联合优化。

Result: 在GasLib-134/135/582上，582节点网络（5000场景训练）压力MAE 1.05 bar（压力范围1.3%），R²=0.981；投影流量R²=0.972；质量平衡残差10⁻⁵–10⁻⁴ Nm³/s；推理<40 ms；负荷能力和分布外压力测试证明鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该PGNN代理模型可作为物理约束的规划加速器，适用于高容量场景筛选与优先级排序，仅在高度局部化的需求集中区域需结合求解器验证。

Abstract: Large-scale gas-network scenario evaluation is a computational bottleneck in integrated energy-system planning, particularly when gas infrastructure interacts with power, heat, hydrogen, and sector-coupling pathways. Conventional nonlinear hydraulic solvers provide reliable feasibility assessment but are costly for stochastic screening, whereas unconstrained learning-based surrogates may produce hydraulically infeasible states. This study develops a physics-informed graph neural network surrogate for steady-state gas-network simulation and feasibility screening. The model uses an edge-centric architecture to predict pipe-level squared-pressure differences and flows. A differentiable projection layer enforces nodal mass conservation on predicted flows, while a Laplacian reconstruction maps edge pressure differences to topologically consistent nodal pressures.
  The framework is evaluated on GasLib-134, GasLib-135, and GasLib-582 using stochastically generated operating scenarios. On the meshed 582-node benchmark trained with 5000 scenarios, the surrogate achieves a pressure mean absolute error of 1.05~bar, corresponding to 1.3\% of the realized pressure range, with $R^2 = 0.981$. Projected-flow predictions reach $R^2 = 0.972$, and mass-balance residuals are reduced to numerical precision, on the order of $10^{-5}$--$10^{-4}$~Nm$^3$/s. Compared with the MYNTS reference solver, inference is reduced from seconds to milliseconds, with the largest benchmark evaluated in less than 40~ms. Loadability and out-of-distribution stress-test evaluations demonstrate robust feasibility screening under high-load conditions, while strongly localized demand concentrations are identified as cases requiring solver-based verification near feasibility limits. The framework provides a physically constrained planning accelerator for high-volume scenario screening and prioritization.

</details>


### [136] [CSCO: A Backside-PDN-Aware Clock-Signal Co-Optimization Framework for Improved PPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13700)
*Zixiao Wang,Leilei Jin,Zhen Zhuang,Rongmei Chen,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: CSCO是一种数据驱动的背侧供电网络感知协同优化框架，通过智能分配前后层资源给时钟和信号线，解决了BSPDN中资源分配权衡和信号完整性退化问题，在不增加屏蔽开销的情况下改善了WNS/TNS、频率和SI鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BSPDN研究要么只移动时钟网络，要么固定时钟树仅优化信号线，未能探索背侧资源分配的全权衡空间；同时BSPDN缺乏前侧电源-地屏蔽导致严重信号完整性退化。

Method: 提出CSCO框架，采用高效搜索策略识别关键线网进行背侧布线，无需重复评估；联合优化时钟与信号在前后层的资源分配，平衡IR-drop、布线拥塞和PPA；利用背侧布线缓解耦合噪声和串扰引起的SI问题。

Result: 实验结果显示WNS/TNS改善、频率提升以及SI鲁棒性增强，且无需额外屏蔽开销。

Conclusion: CSCO通过数据驱动的协同优化有效解决了BSPDN中背侧资源分配权衡和信号完整性退化问题，为先进逻辑节点的BSPDN设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Backside power delivery networks (BSPDN) have emerged as a promising technology for advanced logic nodes to address IR-drop and PPA challenges. While BSPDN introduces additional routing resources on the backside, these resources are limited and must be carefully partitioned between clock and signal nets, creating a critical resource allocation tradeoff. Prior work either moves only the clock network or assumes a fixed clock tree and optimizes only signal nets, failing to explore the tradeoff space of backside resource allocation. Moreover, lacking frontside power-ground shielding, BSPDN introduces severe signal integrity (SI) degradation. We propose CSCO, a data-driven BSPDN-aware co-optimization framework that jointly allocates limited backside resources between clock and signal nets across frontside/backside layers. CSCO employs efficient search strategies to identify critical nets for backside routing without repeated evaluation, navigating the clock-signal allocation tradeoff to balance IR-drop, routing congestion, and PPA. The framework also leverages backside routing to mitigate coupling noise and crosstalk-induced SI issues. Experiments demonstrate improved WNS/TNS, frequency, and SI robustness without additional shielding overhead.

</details>


### [137] [From Forecasts to Auditable Reports: Evidence Contracts for LLM-Assisted Housing-Guarantee Risk Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14026)
*Hyeongcheol Kim,Yoontae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种证据约束的报告流水线，将住房担保风险预测转化为可审计的操作报告。该方法优先监控上尾事件，检索历史先例，组织证据合同，并验证生成声明。在韩国全税存款担保数据上，该流水线显著提升了高风险检测能力，八种LLM实验表明结构化证据提高了报告质量和数值准确性。51名专家评估支持实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将下月住房担保风险预测转化为可审计操作报告至关重要但极具挑战，原因在于上尾事件稀疏、源记录机密且生成的叙述可能扭曲潜在证据。

Method: 提出证据约束报告流水线：(1) 优先监控上尾事件；(2) 检索与预测逻辑一致的历史先例；(3) 组织可接受信息为类型化证据合同；(4) 在分析师审查前验证生成声明。预测模型在原始面板上训练，报告实验使用校准的合成聚合场景。

Result: 所选预测模型在提升高风险检测的同时保持竞争性平均误差。八种LLM实验显示结构化证据始终提高报告质量、数值保真度和声明级基础。51名分析师和领域专家评估中，多数认为报告实用并支持操作试点。

Conclusion: 可靠的LLM辅助报告需要预测模型与结构化证据、显式验证和分析师监督相结合。

Abstract: Translating next-month housing-guarantee risk forecasts into auditable operational reports is essential yet challenging because upper-tail events are sparse, source records are confidential, and generated narratives can distort the underlying evidence. Using monthly South Korean \textit{jeonse} deposit guarantee data from September 2015 to December 2025, we introduce an evidence-constrained reporting pipeline that prioritizes upper-tail monitoring, retrieves historical precedents aligned with the forecasting rationale, organizes admissible information into typed evidence contracts, and verifies generated claims before analyst review. We train and select the forecasting backbone on the original panel, whereas the reporting experiments use synthetic aggregate scenarios calibrated to its empirical ranges and temporal structure. The selected forecasting model substantially improves high-risk detection while retaining competitive average error. Across eight LLMs, structured evidence consistently increases report quality, numerical fidelity, and claim-level grounding. A practitioner evaluation involving 51 analysts and related domain professionals further indicates that the reports support real-world review and decision-making: most participants rated them as practically useful and endorsed an operational pilot. These findings demonstrate that reliable LLM-assisted reporting requires predictive models to be coupled with structured evidence, explicit verification, and analyst oversight.

</details>

### [138] [A Better-than-$e^{1/e}$ Approximation Algorithm for Nash Social Welfare under Additive Valuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13340)
*Vignesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对加性估值下最大化纳什社会福利的 $(e^{1/e} - c)$-近似算法，其中 $c > 0$ 为常数，改进了此前 $e^{1/e}$ 的最佳近似比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳什社会福利（Nash Social Welfare, NSW）是公平分配中的一个重要目标函数，它平衡了效率和公平性。现有的最佳近似比 $e^{1/e}$ 由 Barman, Krishnamurthy 和 Vaish 在 EC 2018 中给出。本文希望进一步缩小与最优解之间的差距。

Method: 本文提出了一种新的近似算法，通过某种技术手段（具体细节未在摘要中展开）将近似比从 $e^{1/e}$ 改进到 $(e^{1/e} - c)$，其中 $c > 0$ 是一个固定的常数。

Result: 该算法实现了 $(e^{1/e} - c)$ 的近似比（$c > 0$），严格优于此前已知的 $e^{1/e}$ 近似比。

Conclusion: 这项工作在加性估值下最大化纳什社会福利的近似算法上取得了突破，通过一个严格的常数改进，表明了该问题的近似比可以突破 $e^{1/e}$ 的壁垒。

Abstract: We present an $(e^{1/e} - c)$-approximation algorithm for maximizing Nash social welfare under additive valuations, for some constant $c > 0$. This result improves upon the previous best-known approximation factor of $e^{1/e}$ [Barman, Krishnamurthy and Vaish, EC 2018].

</details>


### [139] [Auctions with Contract Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13795)
*Xiaolin Bu,Jiarong Jin,Junzhu Ke,Pinyan Lu,Biaoshuai Tao,Xiang Yan,Chunxue Yang,Haikuo Yang,Zhihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种将合约机制融入拍卖的新型模型，其中竞标者的投资影响交易质量，拍卖人通过承诺事后质量奖励来激励投资。在大量竞标者参与且采用线性合约的条件下，最优奖励因子收敛于拍卖人的质量边际收益，即完全将质量价值传递给获胜者，且该结论在二价和一价拍卖中具有收入等价性，显著优于无质量奖励的标准拍卖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拍卖中，竞标者为提升交易质量所投入的成本（如广告质量优化、政府特许权的前期投资）属于沉没成本，若竞标失败则无法收回，这产生了道德风险问题，抑制了竞标者的投资积极性。因此，需要设计一种将事后质量奖励融入拍卖的机制，以激励竞标者进行高效投入。

Method: 将拍卖理论与委托-代理模型自然推广，建立新的分析框架。考虑二价拍卖和一价拍卖，假设竞标者采用对称贝叶斯纳什均衡策略，在大量竞标者参与的条件下研究线性合约（即转移规则中奖励因子与事后实现质量线性相关），推导并优化拍卖人收益最大化的奖励因子。

Result: 最优奖励因子收敛于拍卖人从质量中获得的边际收益，即拍卖人应将质量价值完全传递给获胜者。该结论对二价拍卖和一价拍卖均成立，且满足收入等价定理——只要存在对称贝叶斯纳什均衡，两种拍卖的拍卖人收益相同。相比无质量奖励的标准拍卖，本文设计的合约显著提升了拍卖人收益。

Conclusion: 在竞标者事前投资影响交易质量的拍卖场景中，通过合约将事后质量奖励完全传递给获胜者是最优的收入最大化策略，且这一机制设计在多种拍卖规则下具有鲁棒性，能有效缓解道德风险问题。

Abstract: We consider a new auction model where the bidders' utilities and the auctioneer's revenue depend on a quality factor of the transaction determined by costly and strategic investments of the bidders. Applications of our model include ad auctions, government concessions and crowdsourcing contests. Crucially, these quality-enhancing efforts made by the bidders are often sunk costs incurred prior to the allocation, creating a fundamental moral hazard problem where the risk of losing the auction discourages investments.
  In this paper, we study the design of revenue-maximizing contracts integrated into auctions: the auctioneer commits to a transfer rule that rewards the winner for the ex-post realized quality of the transaction to incentivize higher effort. Our new framework is a natural generalization of both the auction theory and the principal-agent model. We consider both the second-price and the first-price auctions. We show that natural symmetric Bayes Nash equilibria exist in both auctions. Assuming these natural equilibria are played by the bidders and the number of bidders is large, we study linear contracts and derive the optimal reward factor of the transfer rule that maximizes the auctioneer's revenue. As the main result, we show that the optimal reward factor converges to the auctioneer's marginal benefit from the quality, as the number of bidders grows.
